Status: **MAIN_PUSH_BLOCKED**. No main merge/push or production operation performed.

`1874119fa5c7a871840df6a18c071455935f1788`; fresh explicit fetch of all branch refs and PR head refs completed. Plain git fetch alone was insufficient because this checkout has a narrow configured fetch refspec.

## Preservation and content conclusions

- The five task publication heads have exactly the same file trees as their preserved original local branches. They are independent work, not six sequential feature branches.
- The full-validation publication head combines those five task trees plus the manifest fix and subsequent documentation. Main is its ancestor. Its initial publication tree matched the original integrated tree; later differences are documentation only.
- The original full-validation local branch is retained at 12bf8b0. No original refs or commit history were rewritten/deleted by this investigation.

## Missing management integration
Remote `codex/feat-app-next-timetable-management-batch` HEAD `7f72dda9d35334f01cc63b65eebe4b20d938501f` is based on the same main but is not contained in the published full-validation branch.

- It adds period creation/time editing and timetable set creation/edit/archive/unarchive/reactivation, management UI/services/tests and the explicit period-delete-blocked policy. The six published branches do not contain these management files.
- Row CRUD, notices and schedules already exist in main. Applying all six publication branches separately would be unnecessary; use the full-validation result once, together with the separate management changes only after preservation is proven.
- The management branch also adds /CLOUD_HANDOFF.md and /CLAUDE.md.mainpc-old ignore entries. These entries are absent in the published full-validation tree. The files are not tracked in this cloud checkout; ignore entries do not transfer or delete PC files.

## Blocking user-document evidence gap

The cloud checkout has no PC working tree, no CLOUD_HANDOFF.md or CLAUDE.md.mainpc-old, and no identified commit containing the claimed later PC-user-document integration or cloud-cancellation history. The management branch contains its own dated feature/safety handoff, but that is not proof that the PC edits were included. Prior publication commits merged only the five cloud follow-up results, not that PC review. We cannot establish preservation of the requested user edits from the available trees. Per the user instruction, main push is stopped at this gate; no speculative document union or feature merge was performed.

Required evidence to resume: a Git-tracked review commit/branch (or inspectable snapshot/diff) covering the PC changes to AGENTS.md and CURRENT_HANDOFF.md, including the cloud-cancellation and timetable-management records. Ignored personal handoff files should remain local; no secret contents are required.

## PR, protection and CI

GitHub PR-list GraphQL and main-protection REST checks both returned Forbidden. Git PR head discovery found refs/pull/1/head at fdad7896f1b430b5fbc8710c44ebdb55508a4246. Its existence does not establish current PR status or protection policy. CI and protection are NOT VERIFIED; no bypass attempted.

## Validation status

Historical full-validation source checks: root test/typecheck/builds, release 33/33 and 18/18, Functions 559/559 + 11 flows, active Rules 171/171, configured 9/9, candidate 30/30 and Browser 20/20. Those results exclude the newly discovered management branch. A final combined candidate has not been established, so all requested final automatic suites are NOT RUN (preservation gate blocked), not PASS. Only read-only tree/ancestry comparisons and report diff checks were executed now. No tests were weakened, skipped in their definitions or deleted.

## Branch disposition

- Preserve all six publication branches and original local branches. They are not reflected in main yet.
- Keep the timetable-management branch: required independent functionality. Keep row-CRUD branch as an already-integrated recovery point.
- The other historical remote branches and PR head remain investigation/recovery refs; do not merge or delete them indiscriminately.
- One worktree: /workspace/My-Web. This investigation branch contains documentation only. No branch or worktree deletion.

## Cross-device guidance

Main remains at the SHA above. `git pull --ff-only origin main` therefore does not deliver the pending management integration yet. On PC/laptop inspect git status first, preserve local edits explicitly, then switch to main and pull only when the tree/branch is safe. Do not reset, clean or overwrite local files to make pull succeed. Cloud cannot attest to PC uncommitted-file preservation.

Existing risks remain: dependency advisories, legacy arbitrary-ID timetable races, school scope restrictions, mobile/print parity and physical/long-offline QA. Period deletion must remain fail-closed; IAM HOLD remains. Firebase deploy/data changes and environment publication NOT PERFORMED.
